Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "nametag", "tag" },
	Description = "Set a custom nametag above your head",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Text", Type = "String" },
	},
	Task = function(Text)
		local Head = Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
		if not Head then return "Nametag", "No head found" end
		local Old = Head:FindFirstChild("CMD_Nametag")
		if Old then Old:Destroy() end
		if not Text or Text == "" then return "Nametag", "Nametag removed" end
		local BG = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
		BG.Name = "CMD_Nametag"
		BG.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 50)
		BG.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
		BG.Adornee = Head
		BG.AlwaysOnTop = true
		BG.Parent = Head
		local Label = Instance.new("TextLabel", BG)
		Label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		Label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		Label.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		Label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
		Label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		Label.TextScaled = true
		Label.Text = Text
		return "Nametag", Format("Nametag set to: %s", Text)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "removenametag", "untag" },
	Description = "Remove custom nametag",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		local Head = Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
		if Head then
			local Tag = Head:FindFirstChild("CMD_Nametag")
			if Tag then Tag:Destroy() end
		end
		return "Nametag", "Nametag removed"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "colorshift" },
	Description = "Color shift your character",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "R", Type = "Number" },
		{ Name = "G", Type = "Number" },
		{ Name = "B", Type = "Number" },
	},
	Task = function(R, G, B)
		R = tonumber(R) or 255
		G = tonumber(G) or 0
		B = tonumber(B) or 0
		local NewColor = Color3.fromRGB(R, G, B)
		for _, Part in next, Character:GetDescendants() do
			if Part:IsA("BasePart") then
				Part.Color = NewColor
			end
		end
		return "ColorShift", Format("Shifted color to %d,%d,%d", R, G, B)
	end,
})

		return "SilentAim", "Silent aim enabled"

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "rageaim", "raim" },
	Description = "Aim to multiple targets rapidly",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Speed", Type = "Number" },
	},
	Task = function(Speed)
		if Get("RageAim") then
			Add("RageAim", false)
			return "RageAim", "Rage aim disabled"
		end
		Speed = tonumber(Speed) or 0.05
		Add("RageAim", true)
		Cleaner:Add("RageAim", Spawn(function()
			while Get("RageAim") do
				for _, Plr in next, Services.Players:GetPlayers() do
					if not Get("RageAim") then break end
					if Plr == LocalPlayer then continue end
					local Head = Plr.Character and Plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
					if Head then
						Camera.CFrame = CFrame.new(Camera.CFrame.Position, Head.Position)
					end
				end
				Wait(Speed)
			end
		end))
		return "RageAim", "Rage aim enabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "unrageaim", "unraim" },
	Description = "Disable rage aim",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		Add("RageAim", false)
		return "RageAim", "Rage aim disabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "triggerbot", "tbot" },
	Description = "Auto click when hovering a player",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		if Get("TriggerBot") then
			Add("TriggerBot", false)
			return "TriggerBot", "Triggerbot disabled"
		end
		Add("TriggerBot", true)
		Cleaner:Add("TriggerBot", Connect(Services.Run.Heartbeat, function()
			if not Get("TriggerBot") then return end
			local Target = Mouse.Target
			if Target then
				local Plr = Services.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Target.Parent)
				if Plr and Plr ~= LocalPlayer then
					if mouse1click then mouse1click() end
				end
			end
		end))
		return "TriggerBot", "Triggerbot enabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "instakillreach", "ikr" },
	Description = "Kill players in reach",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Range", Type = "Number" },
	},
	Task = function(Range)
		if Get("InstaKillReach") then
			Add("InstaKillReach", false)
			return "InstaKillReach", "Instakill reach disabled"
		end
		Range = tonumber(Range) or 20
		Add("InstaKillReach", true)
		Cleaner:Add("InstaKillReach", Connect(Services.Run.Heartbeat, function()
			if not Get("InstaKillReach") then return end
			for _, Plr in next, Services.Players:GetPlayers() do
				if Plr == LocalPlayer then continue end
				local PlrRoot = GetRoot(Plr)
				if PlrRoot and (Root.Position - PlrRoot.Position).Magnitude <= Range then
					local PlrHum = GetHumanoid(Plr)
					if PlrHum then PlrHum.Health = 0 end
				end
			end
		end))
		return "InstaKillReach", Format("Instakill reach enabled (range: %s)", Range)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "uninstakillreach", "unikr" },
	Description = "Disable instakill reach",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		Add("InstaKillReach", false)
		return "InstaKillReach", "Instakill reach disabled"
	end,
})

	Aliases = { "touchreach" },
	Description = "Kill players on touch",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		if Get("TouchReach") then
			Add("TouchReach", false)
			return "TouchReach", "Touch reach disabled"
		end
		Add("TouchReach", true)
		Cleaner:Add("TouchReach", Connect(Root.Touched, function(Part)
			if not Get("TouchReach") then return end
			local Plr = Services.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Part.Parent)
			if Plr and Plr ~= LocalPlayer then
				local H = GetHumanoid(Plr)
				if H then H.Health = 0 end
			end
		end))
		return "TouchReach", "Touch reach enabled"
	end,
})

	Aliases = { "untouchreach" },
	Description = "Disable touch reach",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		Add("TouchReach", false)
		return "TouchReach", "Touch reach disabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "aura", "killaura" },
	Description = "Kill all players in range",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Range", Type = "Number" },
		{ Name = "Speed", Type = "Number" },
	},
	Task = function(Range, Speed)
		if Get("Aura") then
			Add("Aura", false)
			return "Aura", "Kill aura disabled"
		end
		Range = tonumber(Range) or 15
		Speed = tonumber(Speed) or 0.1
		Add("Aura", true)
		Cleaner:Add("Aura", Spawn(function()
			while Get("Aura") do
				for _, Plr in next, Services.Players:GetPlayers() do
					if not Get("Aura") then break end
					if Plr == LocalPlayer then continue end
					local PR = GetRoot(Plr)
					local PH = GetHumanoid(Plr)
					if PR and PH and (Root.Position - PR.Position).Magnitude <= Range then
						PH.Health = 0
					end
				end
				Wait(Speed)
			end
		end))
		return "Aura", Format("Kill aura enabled (range: %s)", Range)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "unaura", "unkillaura" },
	Description = "Disable kill aura",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		Add("Aura", false)
		return "Aura", "Kill aura disabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "flingaura", "faura" },
	Description = "Fling all players in range",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Range", Type = "Number" },
	},
	Task = function(Range)
		if Get("FlingAura") then
			Add("FlingAura", false)
			return "FlingAura", "Fling aura disabled"
		end
		Range = tonumber(Range) or 15
		Add("FlingAura", true)
		Cleaner:Add("FlingAura", Connect(Services.Run.Heartbeat, function()
			if not Get("FlingAura") then return end
			for _, Plr in next, Services.Players:GetPlayers() do
				if Plr == LocalPlayer then continue end
				local PR = GetRoot(Plr)
				if PR and (Root.Position - PR.Position).Magnitude <= Range then
					PR.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
						math.random(-500, 500),
						math.random(200, 500),
						math.random(-500, 500)
					)
				end
			end
		end))
		return "FlingAura", Format("Fling aura enabled (range: %s)", Range)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "unflingaura" },
	Description = "Disable fling aura",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		Add("FlingAura", false)
		return "FlingAura", "Fling aura disabled"
	end,
})

-- GHOSTMODE / INVISIBLE
-- ============================

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "ghostmode", "ghost2" },
	Description = "Make your character intangible (walk through players)",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		if Get("GhostMode") then
			Add("GhostMode", false)
			Cleaner:Clean("GhostMode")
			for _, Part in next, Character:GetDescendants() do
				if Part:IsA("BasePart") and Part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
					Part.CanCollide = true
				end
			end
			return "GhostMode", "Ghost mode disabled"
		end
		Add("GhostMode", true)
		Cleaner:Add("GhostMode", Connect(Services.Run.Stepped, function()
			if not Get("GhostMode") then return end
			for _, Part in next, Character:GetDescendants() do
				if Part:IsA("BasePart") and Part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
					Part.CanCollide = false
				end
			end
		end))
		return "GhostMode", "Ghost mode enabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "unghostmode", "unghost2" },
	Description = "Disable ghost mode",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		Add("GhostMode", false)
		Cleaner:Clean("GhostMode")
		for _, Part in next, Character:GetDescendants() do
			if Part:IsA("BasePart") and Part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
				Part.CanCollide = true
			end
		end
		return "GhostMode", "Ghost mode disabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "invisible2", "invis2" },
	Description = "Make character invisible (v2)",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		if Get("Invisible2") then
			Add("Invisible2", false)
			for _, Part in next, Character:GetDescendants() do
				if Part:IsA("BasePart") then
					Part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
				end
			end
			return "Invisible2", "Invisible disabled"
		end
		Add("Invisible2", true)
		Cleaner:Add("Invisible2", Connect(Services.Run.RenderStepped, function()
			if not Get("Invisible2") then return end
			for _, Part in next, Character:GetDescendants() do
				if Part:IsA("BasePart") then
					Part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
				end
			end
		end))
		return "Invisible2", "Invisible enabled"
	end,
})

-- ============================
-- BHOP / MOVEMENT TRICKS
-- ============================

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "bhop", "bunnyhop" },
	Description = "Bunny hop",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Power", Type = "Number" },
	},
	Task = function(Power)
		if Get("Bhop") then
			Add("Bhop", false)
			return "Bhop", "Bhop disabled"
		end
		Power = tonumber(Power) or 60
		Add("Bhop", true)
		Cleaner:Add("Bhop", Connect(Services.Run.Heartbeat, function()
			if not Get("Bhop") then return end
			if Humanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed
				or Humanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running then
				Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
			end
		end))
		return "Bhop", Format("Bhop enabled (power: %s)", Power)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "unbhop", "unbunnyhop" },
	Description = "Disable bhop",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		Add("Bhop", false)
		return "Bhop", "Bhop disabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "supermanfly", "superman" },
	Description = "Fly without tools",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Speed", Type = "Number" },
	},
	Task = function(Speed)
		if Get("SuperFly") then
			Add("SuperFly", false)
			Cleaner:Clean("SuperFly")
			Humanoid.PlatformStand = false
			return "SuperFly", "Superman fly disabled"
		end
		Speed = tonumber(Speed) or 50
		Add("SuperFly", true)
		Humanoid.PlatformStand = true
		local BV = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
		BV.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
		BV.Velocity = Vector3.zero
		BV.Parent = Root
		local BG = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
		BG.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
		BG.P = 1e6
		BG.CFrame = Root.CFrame
		BG.Parent = Root
		Add("SuperFlyBV", BV)
		Add("SuperFlyBG", BG)
		Cleaner:Add("SuperFly", Connect(Services.Run.RenderStepped, function()
			if not Get("SuperFly") then return end
			BG.CFrame = Camera.CFrame
			local Dir = Vector3.zero
			if Services.Input:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then Dir = Dir + Camera.CFrame.LookVector end
			if Services.Input:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then Dir = Dir - Camera.CFrame.LookVector end
			if Services.Input:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then Dir = Dir - Camera.CFrame.RightVector end
			if Services.Input:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then Dir = Dir + Camera.CFrame.RightVector end
			if Services.Input:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.E) then Dir = Dir + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
			if Services.Input:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Q) then Dir = Dir - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
			BV.Velocity = Dir.Magnitude > 0 and Dir.Unit * Speed or Vector3.zero
		end))
		return "SuperFly", Format("Superman fly enabled (speed: %s)", Speed)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "unsupermanfly", "unsuperman" },
	Description = "Disable superman fly",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		Add("SuperFly", false)
		Cleaner:Clean("SuperFly")
		local BV = Get("SuperFlyBV")
		local BG = Get("SuperFlyBG")
		if BV then BV:Destroy() end
		if BG then BG:Destroy() end
		Humanoid.PlatformStand = false
		return "SuperFly", "Superman fly disabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "betterfly2", "bfly2" },
	Description = "Smooth fly mode with WASD",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Speed", Type = "Number" },
	},
	Task = function(Speed)
		if Get("BetterFly") then
			Add("BetterFly", false)
			Cleaner:Clean("BetterFly")
			SetFly(false)
			Humanoid.WalkSpeed = 16
			return "BetterFly", "Better fly disabled"
		end
		Speed = tonumber(Speed) or 50
		SetFly(true, Speed / 16)
		Add("BetterFly", true)
		return "BetterFly", Format("Better fly enabled (speed: %s)", Speed)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "unbetterfly2", "unbfly2" },

-- ============================
-- PART CONTROL
-- ============================

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "partcontrol", "pc" },
	Description = "Control parts with your character",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Part", Type = "String" },
	},
	Task = function(PartName)
		if Get("PartControl") then
			Add("PartControl", false)
			Cleaner:Clean("PartControl")
			return "PartControl", "Part control disabled"
		end
		local TargetPart = PartName and workspace:FindFirstChild(PartName)
			or Mouse.Target
		if not TargetPart then return "PartControl", "No part found" end
		Add("PartControl", true)
		Add("PartControlTarget", TargetPart)
		Cleaner:Add("PartControl", Connect(Services.Run.Heartbeat, function()
			if not Get("PartControl") then return end
			local T = Get("PartControlTarget")
			if T then T.CFrame = Root.CFrame end
		end))
		return "PartControl", Format("Controlling: %s", TargetPart.Name)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "unpartcontrol", "unpc" },
	Description = "Stop part control",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		Add("PartControl", false)
		Cleaner:Clean("PartControl")
		Add("PartControlTarget", nil)
		return "PartControl", "Part control disabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "partstorm", "ps" },
	Description = "Spawn a storm of parts around you",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Count", Type = "Number" },
		{ Name = "Size", Type = "Number" },
	},
	Task = function(Count, Size)
		if Get("PartStorm") then
			Add("PartStorm", false)
			Cleaner:Clean("PartStorm")
			for _, V in next, workspace:GetChildren() do
				if V.Name == "CMD_StormPart" then V:Destroy() end
			end
			return "PartStorm", "Part storm disabled"
		end
		Count = math.clamp(tonumber(Count) or 20, 1, 50)
		Size = tonumber(Size) or 2
		Add("PartStorm", true)
		for i = 1, Count do
			local Part = Instance.new("Part")
			Part.Name = "CMD_StormPart"
			Part.Size = Vector3.new(Size, Size, Size)
			Part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
			Part.BrickColor = BrickColor.Random()
			Part.CFrame = Root.CFrame * CFrame.new(
				math.random(-10, 10), math.random(-5, 5), math.random(-10, 10)
			)
			Part.Parent = workspace
		end
		Cleaner:Add("PartStorm", Connect(Services.Run.Heartbeat, function()
			if not Get("PartStorm") then return end
			local Angle = os.clock() * 3
			local Idx = 0
			for _, Part in next, workspace:GetChildren() do
				if Part.Name == "CMD_StormPart" then
					Idx = Idx + 1
					local A = Angle + (Idx / Count) * math.pi * 2
					Part.CFrame = CFrame.new(
						Root.Position + Vector3.new(math.cos(A) * 8, math.sin(Idx) * 3, math.sin(A) * 8)
					)
				end
			end
		end))
		return "PartStorm", Format("Part storm enabled (%d parts)", Count)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "unpartstorm" },
	Description = "Disable part storm",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		Add("PartStorm", false)
		Cleaner:Clean("PartStorm")
		for _, V in next, workspace:GetChildren() do
			if V.Name == "CMD_StormPart" then V:Destroy() end
		end
		return "PartStorm", "Part storm disabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "parttrap", "ptrap" },
	Description = "Trap a player with parts",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Player", Type = "Player" },
	},
	Task = function(Players)
		local Target = Players and Players[1]
		if not Target then return "PartTrap", "No player found" end
		local TR = GetRoot(Target)
		if not TR then return "PartTrap", "Target has no root" end
		local Offsets = {
			Vector3.new(3.5, 0, 0), Vector3.new(-3.5, 0, 0),
			Vector3.new(0, 0, 3.5), Vector3.new(0, 0, -3.5),
			Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), Vector3.new(0, -1, 0),
		}
		for _, Offset in next, Offsets do
			local Part = Instance.new("Part")
			Part.Name = "CMD_Trap"
			Part.Size = Vector3.new(7, 7, 1)
			Part.Anchored = true
			Part.CFrame = CFrame.new(TR.Position + Offset)
			Part.Transparency = 0.5
			Part.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Bright red")
			Part.Parent = workspace
		end
		return "PartTrap", Format("Trapped %s", Target.Name)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "cleartraps", "removetrap" },
	Description = "Remove all traps",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		local Count = 0
		for _, V in next, workspace:GetDescendants() do
			if V.Name == "CMD_Trap" then V:Destroy(); Count += 1 end
		end
		return "ClearTraps", Format("Removed %d trap(s)", Count)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "blackhole", "bh2" },
	Description = "Create a black hole that sucks players",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Power", Type = "Number" },
		{ Name = "Range", Type = "Number" },
	},
	Task = function(Power, Range)
		if Get("Blackhole") then
			Add("Blackhole", false)
			Cleaner:Clean("Blackhole")
			return "Blackhole", "Blackhole disabled"
		end
		Power = tonumber(Power) or 200
		Range = tonumber(Range) or 50
		Add("Blackhole", true)
		local Position = Root.Position
		Cleaner:Add("Blackhole", Connect(Services.Run.Heartbeat, function()
			if not Get("Blackhole") then return end
			for _, Plr in next, Services.Players:GetPlayers() do
				if Plr == LocalPlayer then continue end
				local PR = GetRoot(Plr)
				if PR then
					local Dist = (Position - PR.Position).Magnitude
					if Dist <= Range then
						local Dir = (Position - PR.Position).Unit
						PR.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Dir * Power
					end
				end
			end
		end))
		return "Blackhole", Format("Blackhole active (power: %s, range: %s)", Power, Range)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "unblackhole" },
	Description = "Disable blackhole",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		Add("Blackhole", false)
		Cleaner:Clean("Blackhole")
		return "Blackhole", "Blackhole disabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "addalias2" },
	Description = "Add alias for a command",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Command", Type = "String" },
		{ Name = "Alias", Type = "String" },
	},
	Task = function(CmdName, Alias)
		if not CmdName or not Alias then return "AddAlias", "Provide command and alias" end
		local Found = Command.Find(CmdName)
		if Found then
			table.insert(Found[1], Alias)
			return "AddAlias", Format("Added alias '%s' for '%s'", Alias, CmdName)
		end
		return "AddAlias", Format("Command '%s' not found", CmdName)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "aliases2" },
	Description = "Show all aliases of a command",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Command", Type = "String" },
	},
	Task = function(CmdName)
		if not CmdName then return "Aliases", "Provide a command name" end
		local Found = Command.Find(CmdName)
		if Found then
			return "Aliases", Format("%s: %s", CmdName, table.concat(Found[1], ", "))
		end
		return "Aliases", Format("Command '%s' not found", CmdName)
	end,
})

	Description = "Bring a player to you",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Player", Type = "Player" },
	},
	Task = function(Players)
		for _, Player in next, Players do
			local TR = GetRoot(Player)
			if TR then
				TR.CFrame = Root.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -3)
			end
		end
		return "BringToMe", "Players brought to you"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "seatbring2" },
	Description = "Teleport to a player via seat",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Player", Type = "Player" },
	},
	Task = function(Players)
		local Target = Players and Players[1]
		if not Target then return end
		local TR = GetRoot(Target)
		if not TR then return end
		local Seat = Instance.new("Seat")
		Seat.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
		Seat.Transparency = 1
		Seat.CFrame = TR.CFrame
		Seat.Parent = workspace
		Seat:Sit(Humanoid)
		Wait(0.5)
		Seat:Destroy()
		return "SeatBring", Format("Teleported to %s via seat", Target.Name)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "grabtp2" },
	Description = "TP to every player rapidly (grab TP)",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Speed", Type = "Number" },
	},
	Task = function(Speed)
		if Get("GrabTP") then
			Add("GrabTP", false)
			return "GrabTP", "Grab TP disabled"
		end
		Speed = tonumber(Speed) or 0.1
		Add("GrabTP", true)
		Cleaner:Add("GrabTP", Spawn(function()
			while Get("GrabTP") do
				for _, Plr in next, Services.Players:GetPlayers() do
					if not Get("GrabTP") then break end
					if Plr == LocalPlayer then continue end
					local TR = GetRoot(Plr)
					if TR then Root.CFrame = TR.CFrame end
				end
				Wait(Speed)
			end
		end))
		return "GrabTP", Format("Grab TP enabled (speed: %ss)", Speed)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "showfov" },
	Description = "Show a FOV circle",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Radius", Type = "Number" },
	},
	Task = function(Radius)
		if Get("ShowFOV") then
			Add("ShowFOV", false)
			local FG = Get("FOVGui")
			if FG then FG:Destroy() end
			return "ShowFOV", "FOV circle hidden"
		end
		Radius = tonumber(Radius) or 100
		local Gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
		Gui.Name = "CMD_FOVCircle"
		Gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
		Gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
		Gui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")
		local Frame = Instance.new("Frame", Gui)
		Frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		Frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
		Frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, Radius * 2, 0, Radius * 2)
		Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		Instance.new("UICorner", Frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
		local Stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", Frame)
		Stroke.Thickness = 2
		Stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)
		Add("ShowFOV", true)
		Add("FOVGui", Gui)
		return "ShowFOV", Format("FOV circle shown (radius: %s)", Radius)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "hidecoreguixxx", "hidegui" },
	Description = "Hide a CoreGui element",

		{ Name = "Name", Type = "String" },

			Target.Enabled = false
			return "HideGui", Format("Hidden: %s", Name)
		end
		return "HideGui", Format("Not found: %s", Name)
	end,
})

	Aliases = { "logmodules", "modules" },
	Description = "List all required modules in the game",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		local Mods = {}
		for _, V in next, game:GetDescendants() do
			if V:IsA("ModuleScript") then
				table.insert(Mods, V:GetFullName())
			end
		end
		print(table.concat(Mods, "\n"))
		return "LogModules", Format("Logged %d modules (see console)", #Mods)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "ping2", "nping" },
	Description = "Show numeric ping in studs per second delay",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		local Ping = math.floor(LocalPlayer:GetNetworkPing() * 1000)
		if Ping < 100 then
			return "Ping", Format("Good ping: %dms", Ping)
		elseif Ping < 200 then
			return "Ping", Format("Average ping: %dms", Ping)
		else
			return "Ping", Format("Bad ping: %dms", Ping)
		end
	end,
})

	Aliases = { "illusion" },
	Description = "Create an illusion of yourself at your position",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		Character.Archivable = true
		local Clone = Character:Clone()
		Clone:PivotTo(Root.CFrame)
		for _, V in next, Clone:GetDescendants() do
			if V:IsA("Script") or V:IsA("LocalScript") or V:IsA("AnimationController") then
				V:Destroy()
			end
		end
		Clone.Parent = workspace
		Add("IllusionClone", Clone)
		return "Illusion", "Illusion created"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "hitsound" },
	Description = "Play a sound on kill",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "SoundID", Type = "String" },
	},
	Task = function(SoundID)
		if Get("HitSound") then
			Add("HitSound", false)
			Cleaner:Clean("HitSound")
			return "HitSound", "Hitsound disabled"
		end
		SoundID = SoundID or "131961136"
		Add("HitSound", true)
		local Sound = Instance.new("Sound")
		Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://" .. SoundID
		Sound.Volume = 1
		Sound.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
		Add("HitSoundObj", Sound)
		for _, Plr in next, Services.Players:GetPlayers() do
			if Plr == LocalPlayer then continue end
			Cleaner:Add("HitSound", Connect(Plr.CharacterAdded, function()
				local H = GetHumanoid(Plr)
				if H then
					Cleaner:Add("HitSound", Connect(H.Died, function()
						Sound:Play()
					end))
				end
			end))
		end
		return "HitSound", Format("Hitsound enabled (ID: %s)", SoundID)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "unhitsound" },
	Description = "Disable hitsound",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		Add("HitSound", false)
		Cleaner:Clean("HitSound")
		local S = Get("HitSoundObj")
		if S then S:Destroy(); Add("HitSoundObj", nil) end
		return "HitSound", "Hitsound disabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "mousefling2" },
	Description = "Fling using mouse position",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Power", Type = "Number" },
	},
	Task = function(Power)
		if Get("MouseFling") then
			Add("MouseFling", false)
			return "MouseFling", "Mouse fling disabled"
		end
		Power = tonumber(Power) or 500
		Add("MouseFling", true)
		Cleaner:Add("MouseFling", Connect(Services.Run.Heartbeat, function()
			if not Get("MouseFling") then return end
			local Target = Mouse.Target
			if not Target then return end
			local Plr = Services.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Target.Parent)
			if Plr and Plr ~= LocalPlayer then
				local PR = GetRoot(Plr)
				if PR then
					local Dir = (PR.Position - Root.Position).Unit
					PR.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Dir * Power + Vector3.new(0, Power / 2, 0)
				end
			end
		end))
		return "MouseFling", Format("Mouse fling enabled (power: %s)", Power)
	end,
})

	Aliases = { "unmousefling2" },

		Add("MouseFling", false)

-- ============================
-- NPC RELATED
-- ============================

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "controlnpc", "cnpc" },
	Description = "Control a nearby NPC",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		if Get("ControlNPC") then
			Add("ControlNPC", false)
			Cleaner:Clean("ControlNPC")
			if LocalPlayer.Character ~= Character then
				LocalPlayer.Character = Character
			end
			return "ControlNPC", "NPC control disabled"
		end
		local Closest, ClosestDist = nil, math.huge
		for _, Model in next, workspace:GetDescendants() do
			if Model:IsA("Model") and Model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
				if not Services.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Model) and Model ~= Character then
					local NRoot = Model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if NRoot then
						local Dist = (Root.Position - NRoot.Position).Magnitude
						if Dist < ClosestDist then
							ClosestDist = Dist
							Closest = Model
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		if not Closest then return "ControlNPC", "No NPC found nearby" end
		Add("ControlNPC", true)
		LocalPlayer.Character = Closest
		return "ControlNPC", Format("Controlling NPC: %s", Closest.Name)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "uncontrolnpc", "uncnpc" },
	Description = "Stop controlling NPC",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		Add("ControlNPC", false)
		Cleaner:Clean("ControlNPC")
		LocalPlayer.Character = Character
		return "ControlNPC", "Returned to own character"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "bringnpc", "bnpc" },
	Description = "Bring nearest NPC to you",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Count", Type = "Number" },
	},
	Task = function(Count)
		Count = tonumber(Count) or 1
		local Found = 0
		for _, Model in next, workspace:GetDescendants() do
			if Found >= Count then break end
			if Model:IsA("Model") and Model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
				if not Services.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Model) then
					local NRoot = Model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if NRoot then
						NRoot.CFrame = Root.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -4 - Found)
						Found += 1
					end
				end
			end
		end
		return "BringNPC", Format("Brought %d NPC(s)", Found)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "killnpc", "knpc" },
	Description = "Kill all NPCs",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		local Count = 0
		for _, Model in next, workspace:GetDescendants() do
			if Model:IsA("Model") then
				local H = Model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if H and not Services.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Model) then
					H.Health = 0
					Count += 1
				end
			end
		end
		return "KillNPC", Format("Killed %d NPC(s)", Count)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "flingnpc", "fnpc" },
	Description = "Fling all NPCs",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Power", Type = "Number" },
	},
	Task = function(Power)
		Power = tonumber(Power) or 1000
		local Count = 0
		for _, Model in next, workspace:GetDescendants() do
			if Model:IsA("Model") then
				local NRoot = Model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local H = Model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if NRoot and H and not Services.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Model) then
					NRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
						math.random(-Power, Power),
						Power,
						math.random(-Power, Power)
					)
					Count += 1
				end
			end
		end
		return "FlingNPC", Format("Flung %d NPC(s)", Count)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "voidnpc", "vnpc" },
	Description = "Send all NPCs to the void",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		local Count = 0
		for _, Model in next, workspace:GetDescendants() do
			if Model:IsA("Model") then
				local NRoot = Model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local H = Model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if NRoot and H and not Services.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Model) then
					NRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -10000, 0)
					Count += 1
				end
			end
		end
		return "VoidNPC", Format("Voided %d NPC(s)", Count)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "clickflingnpc", "cfnpc2" },
	Description = "Fling NPC you click on",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Power", Type = "Number" },
	},
	Task = function(Power)
		if Get("ClickFlingNPC") then
			Add("ClickFlingNPC", false)
			Cleaner:Clean("ClickFlingNPC")
			return "ClickFlingNPC", "Click fling NPC disabled"
		end
		Power = tonumber(Power) or 1000
		Add("ClickFlingNPC", true)
		Cleaner:Add("ClickFlingNPC", Connect(Mouse.Button1Down, function()
			if not Get("ClickFlingNPC") then return end
			local Target = Mouse.Target
			if not Target then return end
			local Model = Target:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
			if Model then
				local H = Model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local NR = Model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if H and NR and not Services.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Model) then
					NR.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
						math.random(-Power, Power), Power,
						math.random(-Power, Power)
					)
				end
			end
		end))
		return "ClickFlingNPC", Format("Click fling NPC enabled (power: %s)", Power)
	end,
})

-- ============================
-- ANTI COMMANDS
-- ============================

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "antiteleport", "anttp" },
	Description = "Prevent being teleported",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		if Get("AntiTP") then
			Add("AntiTP", false)
			Cleaner:Clean("AntiTP")
			return "AntiTP", "Anti-teleport disabled"
		end
		Add("AntiTP", true)
		local LastPos = Root.CFrame
		Cleaner:Add("AntiTP", Connect(Services.Run.Heartbeat, function()
			if not Get("AntiTP") then return end
			local Diff = (Root.Position - LastPos.Position).Magnitude
			if Diff > 50 then
				Root.CFrame = LastPos
			else
				LastPos = Root.CFrame
			end
		end))
		return "AntiTP", "Anti-teleport enabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "unantiteleport", "unanttp" },
	Description = "Disable anti-teleport",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		Add("AntiTP", false)
		Cleaner:Clean("AntiTP")
		return "AntiTP", "Anti-teleport disabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "antipause2", "antilag" },
	Description = "Prevent pausing",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		if Get("AntiPause") then
			Add("AntiPause", false)
			return "AntiPause", "Anti-pause disabled"
		end
		Add("AntiPause", true)
		Cleaner:Add("AntiPause", Connect(game:GetService("GuiService").MenuOpened, function()
			if not Get("AntiPause") then return end
			game:GetService("GuiService"):SetMenuOpen(false)
		end))
		return "AntiPause", "Anti-pause enabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "antiknockback2", "antikb2" },
	Description = "Prevent being knocked back",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		if Get("AntiKB") then
			Add("AntiKB", false)
			Cleaner:Clean("AntiKB")
			return "AntiKB", "Anti-knockback disabled"
		end
		Add("AntiKB", true)
		local SavedVel = Vector3.zero
		Cleaner:Add("AntiKB", Connect(Services.Run.Heartbeat, function()
			if not Get("AntiKB") then return end
			local Vel = Root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
			if Vel.Magnitude > 100 then
				Root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = SavedVel
			else
				SavedVel = Vel
			end
		end))
		return "AntiKB", "Anti-knockback enabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "unantikb2" },
	Description = "Disable anti-knockback",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		Add("AntiKB", false)
		Cleaner:Clean("AntiKB")
		return "AntiKB", "Anti-knockback disabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "antikill2", "antideath" },
	Description = "Prevent being killed",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		if Get("AntiKill") then
			Add("AntiKill", false)
			Cleaner:Clean("AntiKill")
			return "AntiKill", "Anti-kill disabled"
		end
		Add("AntiKill", true)
		Cleaner:Add("AntiKill", Connect(Humanoid.Died, function()
			if not Get("AntiKill") then return end
			Humanoid.Health = Humanoid.MaxHealth
		end))
		Cleaner:Add("AntiKill", Connect(Services.Run.Heartbeat, function()
			if not Get("AntiKill") then return end
			if Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
				Humanoid.Health = Humanoid.MaxHealth
			end
		end))
		return "AntiKill", "Anti-kill enabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "unantikill2" },
	Description = "Disable anti-kill",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		Add("AntiKill", false)
		Cleaner:Clean("AntiKill")
		return "AntiKill", "Anti-kill disabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "antisit2", "nosit2" },
	Description = "Prevent sitting in seats",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		if Get("AntiSit") then
			Add("AntiSit", false)
			Cleaner:Clean("AntiSit")
			return "AntiSit", "Anti-sit disabled"
		end
		Add("AntiSit", true)
		Cleaner:Add("AntiSit", Connect(Humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Sit"), function()
			if not Get("AntiSit") then return end
			if Humanoid.Sit then
				Humanoid.Sit = false
			end
		end))
		return "AntiSit", "Anti-sit enabled"
	end,
})

	Aliases = { "unantisit2" },
	Description = "Disable anti-sit",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		Add("AntiSit", false)
		Cleaner:Clean("AntiSit")
		return "AntiSit", "Anti-sit disabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "anticframeteleport2", "anticframe" },
	Description = "Prevent CFrame teleport exploits",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		if Get("AntiCFrame") then
			Add("AntiCFrame", false)
			Cleaner:Clean("AntiCFrame")
			return "AntiCFrame", "Anti-CFrame teleport disabled"
		end
		Add("AntiCFrame", true)
		local LastCFrame = Root.CFrame
		Cleaner:Add("AntiCFrame", Connect(Services.Run.Heartbeat, function()
			if not Get("AntiCFrame") then return end
			if (Root.Position - LastCFrame.Position).Magnitude > 80 then
				Root.CFrame = LastCFrame
			else
				LastCFrame = Root.CFrame
			end
		end))
		return "AntiCFrame", "Anti-CFrame teleport enabled"
	end,
})

-- ============================
-- EMOTE / DANCE
-- ============================

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "emote2", "emotion" },
	Description = "Play an emote by ID",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "ID", Type = "String" },
	},
	Task = function(ID)
		if not ID then return "Emote", "Provide an emote ID" end
		local Animator = Humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
			or Instance.new("Animator", Humanoid)
		local Anim = Instance.new("Animation")
		Anim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. ID
		Animator:LoadAnimation(Anim):Play()
		return "Emote", Format("Playing emote %s", ID)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "dance2", "danceloop" },
	Description = "Loop a dance animation",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "ID", Type = "String" },
	},
	Task = function(ID)
		if Get("Dance") then
			Add("Dance", false)
			local Track = Get("DanceTrack")
			if Track then Track:Stop() end
			return "Dance", "Dance stopped"
		end
		if not ID then return "Dance", "Provide animation ID" end
		local Animator = Humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
			or Instance.new("Animator", Humanoid)
		local Anim = Instance.new("Animation")
		Anim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. ID
		local Track = Animator:LoadAnimation(Anim)
		Track.Looped = true
		Track:Play()
		Add("Dance", true)
		Add("DanceTrack", Track)
		return "Dance", Format("Dancing with anim %s", ID)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "stopdance", "undance2" },
	Description = "Stop dancing",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		Add("Dance", false)
		local Track = Get("DanceTrack")
		if Track then Track:Stop() end
		return "Dance", "Dance stopped"
	end,
})

-- ============================
-- PART WALK FLING
-- ============================

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "partwalkfling", "pwf" },
	Description = "Fling when touching parts",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		if Get("PartWalkFling") then
			Add("PartWalkFling", false)
			Cleaner:Clean("PartWalkFling")
			return "PartWalkFling", "Part walk fling disabled"
		end
		Add("PartWalkFling", true)
		local Touched = {}
		Cleaner:Add("PartWalkFling", Connect(Root.Touched, function(Part)
			if not Get("PartWalkFling") then return end
			if Touched[Part] then return end
			local Plr = Services.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Part.Parent)
			if Plr then return end
			Touched[Part] = true
			Part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Part.AssemblyLinearVelocity
				+ Vector3.new(math.random(-500, 500), 500, math.random(-500, 500))
			task.delay(0.5, function() Touched[Part] = nil end)
		end))
		return "PartWalkFling", "Part walk fling enabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "unpartwalkfling", "unpwf" },
	Description = "Disable part walk fling",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		Add("PartWalkFling", false)
		Cleaner:Clean("PartWalkFling")
		return "PartWalkFling", "Part walk fling disabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "uafling", "uaf" },
	Description = "Fling unanchored parts",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Power", Type = "Number" },
		{ Name = "Range", Type = "Number" },
	},
	Task = function(Power, Range)
		Power = tonumber(Power) or 1000
		Range = tonumber(Range) or 30
		local Count = 0
		for _, V in next, workspace:GetDescendants() do
			if V:IsA("BasePart") and not V.Anchored
				and (Root.Position - V.Position).Magnitude <= Range then
				if not Services.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(V.Parent) then
					V.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
						math.random(-Power, Power), Power,
						math.random(-Power, Power)
					)
					Count += 1
				end
			end
		end
		return "UAFling", Format("Flung %d unanchored parts", Count)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "spamjump", "jumpspam" },
	Description = "Spam jump",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		if Get("SpamJump") then
			Add("SpamJump", false)
			return "SpamJump", "Spam jump disabled"
		end
		Add("SpamJump", true)
		Cleaner:Add("SpamJump", Connect(Services.Run.Heartbeat, function()
			if not Get("SpamJump") then return end
			Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
		end))
		return "SpamJump", "Spam jump enabled"
	end,

	Aliases = { "unspamjump" },

	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		Add("SpamJump", false)
		return "SpamJump", "Spam jump disabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "tp2", "teleport2" },
	Description = "Teleport to a player",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Player", Type = "Player" },
	},
	Task = function(Players)
		for _, Player in next, Players do
			local TR = GetRoot(Player)
			if TR then
				Root.CFrame = TR.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0)
			end
		end
		return "TP", "Teleported"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "toggletool", "ttool" },
	Description = "Equip/unequip a tool by name",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Tool", Type = "String" },
	},
	Task = function(ToolName)
		if not ToolName then return "ToggleTool", "Provide a tool name" end
		for _, Tool in next, Backpack:GetChildren() do
			if Lower(Tool.Name) == Lower(ToolName) then
				Tool.Parent = Character
				return "ToggleTool", Format("Equipped: %s", Tool.Name)
			end
		end
		for _, Tool in next, Character:GetChildren() do
			if Tool:IsA("Tool") and Lower(Tool.Name) == Lower(ToolName) then
				Tool.Parent = Backpack
				return "ToggleTool", Format("Unequipped: %s", Tool.Name)
			end
		end
		return "ToggleTool", Format("Tool '%s' not found", ToolName)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "equiptool", "equip" },
	Description = "Equip a tool by name",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Tool", Type = "String" },
	},
	Task = function(ToolName)
		if not ToolName then return "Equip", "Provide a tool name" end
		local Target = ToolName == "*" and Backpack:GetChildren() or
			{ Backpack:FindFirstChild(ToolName) }
		local Count = 0
		for _, T in next, Target do
			if T and T:IsA("Tool") then
				T.Parent = Character
				Count += 1
			end
		end
		return "Equip", Format("Equipped %d tool(s)", Count)
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "unequiptools2", "dequip" },
	Description = "Unequip all tools",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		for _, T in next, Character:GetChildren() do
			if T:IsA("Tool") then T.Parent = Backpack end
		end
		return "Unequip", "All tools unequipped"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "itemesp", "toolsesp" },
	Description = "Show ESP on tools in workspace",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		if Get("ItemESP") then
			Add("ItemESP", false)
			Cleaner:Clean("ItemESP")
			for _, V in next, workspace:GetDescendants() do
				if V:IsA("SelectionBox") and V.Name == "CMD_ItemESP" then V:Destroy() end
			end
			return "ItemESP", "Item ESP disabled"
		end
		Add("ItemESP", true)
		Cleaner:Add("ItemESP", Connect(Services.Run.Heartbeat, function()
			if not Get("ItemESP") then return end
			for _, V in next, workspace:GetDescendants() do
				if (V:IsA("Tool") or V:IsA("BackpackItem")) and not V:FindFirstChild("CMD_ItemESP") then
					local Sel = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
					Sel.Name = "CMD_ItemESP"
					Sel.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
					Sel.LineThickness = 0.05
					Sel.Adornee = V:FindFirstChild("Handle") or V
					Sel.Parent = V
				end
			end
		end))
		return "ItemESP", "Item ESP enabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "unitesp", "untoolsesp" },
	Description = "Disable item ESP",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		Add("ItemESP", false)
		Cleaner:Clean("ItemESP")
		for _, V in next, workspace:GetDescendants() do
			if V:IsA("SelectionBox") and V.Name == "CMD_ItemESP" then V:Destroy() end
		end
		return "ItemESP", "Item ESP disabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "itemtracers", "tooltracers" },
	Description = "Draw tracers to tools in workspace",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		if Get("ItemTracers") then
			Add("ItemTracers", false)
			local G = Get("ItemTracerGui")
			if G then G:Destroy(); Add("ItemTracerGui", nil) end
			return "ItemTracers", "Item tracers disabled"
		end
		local Gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
		Gui.Name = "CMD_ItemTracerGui"
		Gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
		Gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
		Gui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")
		Add("ItemTracerGui", Gui)
		Add("ItemTracers", true)
		Cleaner:Add("ItemTracers", Connect(Services.Run.RenderStepped, function()
			if not Get("ItemTracers") then return end
			for _, F in next, Gui:GetChildren() do F:Destroy() end
			local Cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
			for _, V in next, workspace:GetDescendants() do
				if V:IsA("Tool") or V:IsA("BackpackItem") then
					local H = V:FindFirstChild("Handle")
					if H then
						local TP, OS = Cam:WorldToViewportPoint(H.Position)
						if OS then
							local Line = Instance.new("Frame")
							Line.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
							Line.BorderSizePixel = 0
							local VX, VY = Cam.ViewportSize.X / 2, Cam.ViewportSize.Y
							local DX, DY = TP.X - VX, TP.Y - VY
							local Dist = math.sqrt(DX * DX + DY * DY)
							Line.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, Dist)
							Line.Position = UDim2.new(0, VX, 0, VY)
							Line.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(DX, -DY))
							Line.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
							Line.Parent = Gui
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end))
		return "ItemTracers", "Item tracers enabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "unitemtracers" },
	Description = "Disable item tracers",
	Arguments = {},
	Task = function()
		Add("ItemTracers", false)
		local G = Get("ItemTracerGui")
		if G then G:Destroy(); Add("ItemTracerGui", nil) end
		return "ItemTracers", "Item tracers disabled"
	end,
})

Command.Add({
	Aliases = { "bringunanchored", "bua" },
	Description = "Bring all unanchored parts near you",
	Arguments = {
		{ Name = "Range", Type = "Number" },
	},
	Task = function(Range)
		Range = tonumber(Range) or 100
		local Count = 0
		for _, V in next, workspace:GetDescendants() do

				and (Root.Position - V.Position).Magnitude <= Range

				)
				Count += 1
			end
		end
		return "BringUA", Format("Brought %d unanchored parts", Count)
	end,
})
